ParlaMint-PL_2021-04-14-sejm-28-1.u695	Thank you very much. The Sejm agreed that in the discussion on this item he would listen to five minutes' statements on behalf of clubs and circles. I'm opening a discussion. As the first Mr Frederick Kapinos, Club of Law and Justice.
ParlaMint-PL_2021-04-14-sejm-28-1.u696	Dear Marshal, Dear Minister, High Izbo! I have the honour to present to the High House the position of the Parliamentary Club Law and Justice on the Government draft law amending the Payment Act under direct support schemes and certain other laws, print No 1072. The proposed law provides for the introduction of a number of solutions under the direct support scheme and under the RDP. The project envisages amending the Payment Act under the direct support scheme, the national producer registration system, the farm records and the records of payment applications, support for rural development with the contribution of the European Agricultural Fund for Rural Development in the framework of the RDPs for the period 2007 to 2013 and 2014 to 2020. The amendments made by Implementing Regulation 2021/540 of the Commission of the European Union of 26 March 2021 amending Implementing Regulation No 809/2014 make it necessary to adapt national provisions to them. In accordance with the provisions of the Regulation referred to above, provisions setting a deadline for the submission of applications for direct payments and certain measures within the framework of the RDPs and laying down a deadline for the submission of amendments to those applications shall be deleted from 2021. Member States were required to establish the abovementioned. time limits. In the proposal submitted to the High Chamber, it is proposed to maintain the current deadline, i.e. May 31 of the year concerned. This concerns amendments to the proposal. In addition, the draft law provides for delegations to the Minister for Agriculture and Rural Development to extend the deadline for submitting applications and to amend applications in the event of circumstances which prevent farmers from submitting these documents within the basic time limits, such as. flood, drought, frosts, etc. Further favourable solutions proposed in the bill include: take into account the possibility of using photos taken and transmitted by the website in administrative proceedings using the application made available by ARiMR. Add a provision allowing publication by means of a notice of a list of varieties of fibre hemp which are not eligible for direct payments. They will apply if the THC content of fibre hemp is exceeded. The Act on the national system of producer records, farm records and payment application records proposes solutions to allow the President of the ARiMR to make available free of charge spatial data from the agricultural parcel identification system and the integrated control system for research, environmental and statistical purposes. The draft law also provides for amendments to the Rural Development Support Act with the participation of the European Agricultural Fund for Rural Development under the RDPs for the period 2014 to 2020 to simplify the procedure for the selection of operations and other documents by introducing, in addition to the written form, the possibility of submitting the above mentioned. applications and other documents also by electronic means. Repeal of rules excluding the possibility of re-establishing time limits for RDP activities which, at the current stage of implementation of the programme, hamper the management of RDP financial resources due to the ongoing epidemic situation. In conclusion, it should be noted that the draft law must be urgently examined, as farmers should benefit from the provisions laid down therein as part of the ongoing selection campaign. Moreover, since the draft law contains provisions on the setting of a deadline for amending the direct payment application in the framework of the ongoing 2021 campaign, set at 31 May, the law should enter into force before that date. The absence of national rules in this respect would mean that the rules on the deadline for amending applications would cease to function. However, the draft law could not have been previously addressed to parliamentary work since the implementing regulation of Commission 2021/540 of 26 March 2021 was not published until 29 March 2021. Dear Marshal, High Izbo! Parliamentary Club Law and Justice supports the draft in print 1072 submitted to the High House. Thank you for your attention.

The current rules allow such projects to be carried out without the voice of representatives of municipalities and local communities in the decision-making chain, which often leads to pathological situations. An example of this is the district of Nowosolski situated in the area of my voivodship. Two landfills are currently being rehabilitated in its territory: in Bobrniki and Mirocina Dolny. In fact, the rehabilitation of these landfills is to put garbage in the garbage and cover them with a small layer of soil. E.g. in Bobrovniks the proportion in the case of a 2-meter so-called. The rehabilitation cover is as follows: 1,90 One inch of garbage and one inch of hummus. Of course, the Civic Coalition, which ruled in the Seym of the Voivodeship, gave its consent to such a curiosity solution. It is also worth mentioning in this respect that these works are carried out by small companies from o.o. In both situations, and in many similar situations, because such cases occur throughout the country, the risks associated with such investments at the end are borne by the local government and its residents, who are not previously taken into account in the decision-making process. Therefore, both residents and representatives of municipalities should have a greater impact on how landfills in their area are being rehabilitated. I would therefore like to make proposals for amendments to the law which will give the local authorities the lowest level of opportunity to co-decide on the direction of the rehabilitation work being carried out. These proposals came from representatives of the local local local government unit from the district of Novosol, and are the following: the admission of the mayor, mayor or president of the city competent for the place of waste collection or treatment as a party to proceedings both in the proceedings concerning the closure of landfill and remediation and in the treatment and collection of waste, the addition of a provision setting out the competence of the regional marshal to authorise the collection and treatment of waste in the case of decisions to close the recycling of landfills with the use of waste, the introduction of a public consultation with residents before the decision on the closure and remediation of waste, the repeal of the amendment with the consequence of the authorisation as a party to the proceedings of the general government, the mayor or the president of the city competent for the place of waste collection or treatment, the definition of the specific competences of the regional marshal, the old town, the environmental inspector for the carrying out of checks and the introduction of the obligation to provide for the protection of claims in order to cover the replacement costs also with regard to the management of the landfill - by amending Article. 48a bill. That's what the Confederate project will do. Thank you.
ParlaMint-PL_2021-04-14-sejm-28-1.u717	Thank you. Mr Grzegorz Lorek, Club Law and Justice.
ParlaMint-PL_2021-04-14-sejm-28-1.u718	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! It was once said that a great story is made up of ordinary, small people, and these great people just stand out in the crowd. In the context of this statement, the word "mali" becomes a completely different, opposite meaning. The panteon of piotrkowian, those ordinary little people who during World War II fulfilled their duty to their homeland, and of which it is worth remembering and worth remembering, is large. The representative of this generation was Tadeusz Nowarski, whose next anniversary of death was a few days ago. Tadeusz Nowarski was born in Piotrków in 1907 in a family with patriotic roots. His father was an officer, a doctor in the Polish Legions. He himself joined the army after several years of his studies in junior high school, finishing in 1935. Initially he taught pilot candidates, and 3 years later he was assigned to the 2nd Air Force to the 24th Line Squadron in Kraków. In the September campaign, the German XVI Armoured Corps was attacked by the bomb squad under Radomsk and Częstochowa. On September 15, 1939, he undertook a mission to deliver the chief commander's orders from the area of Dubno by plane to the defending Warsaw. This task, with the total advantage of Germans in the air, was practically suicidal. Not only did Nowarski deliver his orders, but he also managed to return to the so-called area. Romanian foreman. Along with many soldiers after the September defeat, he went on an emigration vagabond in search of the possibility of continuing the fight. In 1940, he began serving in the British 609th RAF division. His English colleagues, unable to pronounce his name correctly, simply called him "Novi. " He was one of the few Churchill spoke of in the battle for England. He was praised and liked by his colleagues. Two German fighter planes were shot down on 13 August 1940. From 1942 he served in the Polish Air Force, first as commander of the 308th Hunting Division and later as commander of the entire 2nd Polish Hunting Wing. In 1944, he was sent to the United States and trained American pilots there. The war ended as a colonel. He did not imagine not to return to Poland. So he returned in 1947, and soon later, under fabricated charges, he was accused and arrested by Communists. Fired after a few years, he never went back to flying. He was a professional, ran a locksmith shop and worked as a taxi driver. He left memories that are still waiting to be published. He died on 2 April 1983 and is buried in the cemetery of Powęzkowski. One of many, out of hundreds, out of thousands. A hero? No, an ordinary man. Thank you.
